# Low-Level Design (LLD)

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Status | Draft, baseline for milestone M1 |
| Owns | SQLite schema (DDL), adapter TypeScript contract, MCP tool schemas, failure taxonomy, workspace/lease algorithm, compliance manifest schema, telemetry span list, supervisor/retry/idempotency details, inner-agent configuration, backend port, tables, call pipeline and task contracts (§13), provider and model affinity table, algorithm and routing-decision fields (§3.2), control-plane MCP tools, digest cursor, needs-attention derivation, budgets, loop breaker, pause, approval-proxy tools, tool profiles and notification-sink contracts (§3.4), retrieval-index configuration, stores, algorithms, tool contract and events (§14), continuity: handoff bundle schema, `as_of`, decision kinds, open-work derivation, prompt rendering and the resume working directory (§15), capacity estimator, `get_capacity` output, per-provider breaker, retry successors and the quota ladder (§16), project work-queue records, schedule syntax, creator-bound admission and CI trigger contracts (§18) |
| Does not own | Requirements ([SRS](../requirements/SRS.md)); run state machine and workflows ([FRD](../requirements/FRD.md)); technology choices and per-provider strategy ([TDD](TDD.md)); component boundaries ([HLD](HLD.md)); scope and sequencing ([ROADMAP](../ROADMAP.md#6-milestones)) |
| Last reconciled | 2026-10-15 |

Implementation status is owned by [ROADMAP §6](../ROADMAP.md#6-milestones); parts of this design are now implemented.
M1 and M2 items are specified in detail; later items are marked "to be detailed in Mx".
Numbers marked *(proposed target)* are starting values, not established constraints. Types and column names are
**Proposed** unless an SRS ID or ADR fixes them.

## 1. Data model

Persistence is one SQLite database in WAL mode, single writer ([ADR-0005](../adr/0005-sqlite-ledger.md), SRS §6).
Conventions:

- **IDs** are ULID text with a type prefix: `sess_`, `run_`, `evt_`, `art_` (fixed by the brief); other prefixes are
  Proposed: `dec_`, `ckp_`, `rte_`, `apv_`, `adv_`, `use_`, `pbd_` (provider binding, added in M2 T2.4). IDs are
  generated by the controller, never by providers (FR-SES-01).
- **Timestamps** are `TEXT` in UTC ISO-8601 with millisecond precision (`2026-10-12T09:30:00.123Z`), set from an
  injectable clock. Lease/expiry comparisons use the same format.
- **JSON columns** hold validated JSON text (`CHECK (json_valid(col))`). Prompts and provider output do not live in JSON
  columns; large payloads go to artifacts (FR-TEL-03).
- All tables are `STRICT`. Foreign keys `ON`. `PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL; synchronous=FULL` (Proposed: durability over
  speed for crash safety, NFR-REL-01); `busy_timeout` set although only one writer exists. `recursive_triggers=ON`
  (Delegated, [ROADMAP Q-96](../ROADMAP.md#73-open-questions-for-zemdregon-not-blocking-m2-unless-noted)): without it
  SQLite's `REPLACE` conflict resolution deletes a colliding row without firing the `BEFORE DELETE` guards below, so
  `INSERT OR REPLACE` could rewrite an append-only row; with it the guard fires and the statement aborts.
- **Append-only** (enforced by `BEFORE UPDATE/DELETE` triggers that `RAISE(ABORT)`): `run_event`, `decision`,
`workspace_checkpoint`, `usage_observation`, `route_decision`, and (M11) `inner_call` and (M12) `rag_record` (FR-SES-05,
FR-RTE-03, FR-CAP-01, FR-INN-15). **Update-once** (pending to decided): `approval`. **Mutable current-state**:
`meta_session`, `run`, `provider_health`, `adapter_version.validated_*`, (M7) `project_affinity`, (M11)
`inner_task_state`, (M13) `oauth_token.revoked`/`revoked_at` and `oauth_client.status`/`decided_at`, and
`artifact.deleted_at` only. Retention GC removes artifact files and worktrees and sets `artifact.deleted_at`; it never
deletes `run_event` rows (Assumption).

```sql
CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (
  version     INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  name        TEXT NOT NULL,
  checksum    TEXT NOT NULL,            -- sha256 of migration file; mismatch aborts startup
  applied_at  TEXT NOT NULL
) STRICT;

CREATE TABLE meta_session (
  id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id LIKE 'sess\_%' ESCAPE '\'),
  objective       TEXT NOT NULL,        -- redacted at admission (ADR-0042)
  constraints_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]' CHECK (json_valid(constraints_json)),  -- redacted at admission
  repo_root       TEXT,                 -- canonical path; NULL for non-Git read-only inspect (FR-WS-06)
  base_commit     TEXT,                 -- first base checkpoint commit
  head_commit     TEXT,                 -- latest post-run checkpoint commit
  open_work_json  TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]' CHECK (json_valid(open_work_json)),
  status          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE','ARCHIVED')),
  lease_run_id    TEXT,                 -- write lease holder, see section 5
  lease_expires_at TEXT,
  version         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,   -- optimistic concurrency
  created_at      TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at      TEXT NOT NULL
) STRICT;

CREATE TABLE provider_binding (          -- provider-native sessions (ADR-0001)
  id               TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  meta_session_id  TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES meta_session(id),
  provider         TEXT NOT NULL,       -- adapter id
  harness          TEXT NOT NULL,       -- equals provider unless fronted (FR-ADP-10)
  backing_provider TEXT,                -- actual backing vendor, when reported
  model            TEXT,
  native_session_id TEXT NOT NULL,      -- taken from structured output only (FR-ADP-04)
  created_at       TEXT NOT NULL,
  last_used_at     TEXT NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE (meta_session_id, provider, native_session_id)
) STRICT;

CREATE TABLE adapter_version (
  id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  provider        TEXT NOT NULL,
  binary_path     TEXT NOT NULL,
  binary_version  TEXT NOT NULL,
  manifest_hash   TEXT,                 -- sha256 of compliance manifest in force
  probe_json      TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(probe_json)),  -- steps of FR-ADP-02, no credentials
  status          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('VALIDATED','UNVERIFIED','DISABLED')),
  first_seen_at   TEXT NOT NULL,
  validated_at    TEXT,
  validated_by    TEXT,                 -- OS user name of operator
  UNIQUE (provider, binary_version, manifest_hash)
) STRICT;

CREATE TABLE run (
  id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id LIKE 'run\_%' ESCAPE '\'),
  meta_session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES meta_session(id),
  parent_run_id   TEXT REFERENCES run(id),          -- previous attempt or continued run
  attempt         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  state           TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('AWAITING_APPROVAL','QUEUED','WAITING_CAPACITY',
                    'PREFLIGHT','LEASED','RUNNING','VERIFYING','FINALIZING',
                    'SUCCEEDED','FAILED','CANCELLED')),   -- states owned by FRD
  failure_code    TEXT,                              -- one code from section 4; NOT NULL iff FAILED
  failure_detail  TEXT,                              -- short, redacted
  provider        TEXT,                              -- NULL until routed
  adapter_version_id TEXT REFERENCES adapter_version(id),
  model           TEXT,
  trust_profile   TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (trust_profile IN ('inspect-untrusted','edit-untrusted',
                    'test-sandboxed','trusted-development','privileged')),
  policy_snapshot_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(policy_snapshot_json)),
  request_json    TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(request_json)),  -- validated request, objective and constraints
                                                     -- redacted (ADR-0042); admission_redactions when > 0
  request_hash    TEXT NOT NULL,
  client_id       TEXT NOT NULL,                     -- configured MCP client (FR-MCP-06)
  idempotency_key TEXT,
  priority        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  worktree_path   TEXT,
  branch          TEXT,
  base_checkpoint_id TEXT,                           -- -> workspace_checkpoint(id)
  post_checkpoint_id TEXT,
  pid             INTEGER,
  pgid            INTEGER,
  proc_start_ticks INTEGER,                          -- guards against PID reuse at reconciliation
  exit_code       INTEGER,
  exit_signal     TEXT,
  next_attempt_at TEXT,                              -- retry / WAITING_CAPACITY wake time
  version         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,        -- optimistic transition guard
  created_at      TEXT NOT NULL,
  started_at      TEXT,
  finished_at     TEXT,
  updated_at      TEXT NOT NULL,
  CHECK ((state = 'FAILED') = (failure_code IS NOT NULL))
) STRICT;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX run_idem ON run(client_id, idempotency_key) WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX run_state ON run(state, priority DESC, created_at);
CREATE INDEX run_session ON run(meta_session_id, created_at);

CREATE TABLE run_event (                -- append-only (FR-TEL-01, FR-SES-05)
  id          TEXT PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id LIKE 'evt\_%' ESCAPE '\'),
  run_id      TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES run(id),
  seq         INTEGER NOT NULL,         -- per-run, gapless, starts at 1
  ts          TEXT NOT NULL,
  type        TEXT NOT NULL,            -- 'state_transition','provider_event','policy','lease', ...
  payload_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(payload_json)),  -- bounded, no raw output
  artifact_id TEXT REFERENCES artifact(id),
  UNIQUE (run_id, seq)
) STRICT;

CREATE TABLE decision (                 -- append-only
  id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  meta_session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES meta_session(id),
  run_id          TEXT REFERENCES run(id),
  decision        TEXT NOT NULL,
  reason_summary  TEXT NOT NULL,        -- observable rationale, not provider reasoning (NFR-PRIV-02)
  created_at      TEXT NOT NULL
) STRICT;

CREATE TABLE artifact (                 -- content-addressed files under <data_dir>/artifacts/ab/cd/<sha256>
  id          TEXT PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id LIKE 'art\_%' ESCAPE '\'),
  run_id      TEXT REFERENCES run(id),
  meta_session_id TEXT REFERENCES meta_session(id),
  kind        TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('provider_stdout','provider_stderr','diff','test_report',
                'changed_files','log_summary','handoff_view')),
  sha256      TEXT NOT NULL,
  size_bytes  INTEGER NOT NULL,
  media_type  TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at  TEXT NOT NULL,
  deleted_at  TEXT                      -- set by retention GC (NFR-DATA-01)
) STRICT;
CREATE INDEX artifact_run ON artifact(run_id, kind);

CREATE TABLE workspace_checkpoint (     -- append-only
  id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  meta_session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES meta_session(id),
  run_id          TEXT REFERENCES run(id),
  kind            TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('base','pre','post')),
  commit_sha      TEXT NOT NULL,
  branch          TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at      TEXT NOT NULL
) STRICT;

CREATE TABLE usage_observation (        -- append-only; reported never merged with estimated (FR-CAP-01)
  id               TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  run_id           TEXT REFERENCES run(id),
  provider         TEXT NOT NULL,
  harness          TEXT NOT NULL,
  backing_provider TEXT,
  model            TEXT,
  source           TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (source IN ('provider_reported','controller_estimated')),
  metrics_json     TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(metrics_json)), -- tokens, cost as reported, rate-limit signals, reset_at
  observed_at      TEXT NOT NULL
) STRICT;
CREATE INDEX usage_provider_time ON usage_observation(provider, observed_at);

CREATE TABLE provider_health (          -- mutable, one row per provider; breaker use in §16.4
  provider          TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  auth_state        TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (auth_state IN ('OK','AUTH_REQUIRED','UNKNOWN')),
  version_state     TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (version_state IN ('VALIDATED','UNVERIFIED','ABSENT')),
  breaker_state     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'CLOSED' CHECK (breaker_state IN ('CLOSED','OPEN','HALF_OPEN')),
  breaker_until     TEXT,
  consecutive_failures INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  last_probe_at     TEXT,
  last_failure_code TEXT,
  capacity_json     TEXT CHECK (capacity_json IS NULL OR json_valid(capacity_json)), -- FR-CAP-02, M6
  updated_at        TEXT NOT NULL,
  version           INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
) STRICT;

CREATE TABLE route_decision (           -- append-only (FR-RTE-03); M2 writes eligibility only, M7 adds scores
  id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  run_id          TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES run(id),
  features_json   TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(features_json)),  -- M11: feature entries carry source, model_id, template_version (§13.6)
  weights_json    TEXT CHECK (weights_json IS NULL OR json_valid(weights_json)),
  candidates_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(candidates_json)), -- per candidate: eligible?, reasons, score
  selected_provider TEXT,
  reasons_json    TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(reasons_json)),
  created_at      TEXT NOT NULL
) STRICT;

CREATE TABLE approval (                 -- privileged runs (FR-POL-09), M3
  id            TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  run_id        TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES run(id),  -- the run that requested it; covers its meta-session (§3.1)
  scope_json    TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(scope_json)),   -- canonical privileged_scope (§3.1)
  scope_hash    TEXT NOT NULL,          -- SHA-256 over client, meta-session and scope (§3.1, v2)
  status        TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('PENDING','APPROVED','DENIED','EXPIRED')),
  requested_at  TEXT NOT NULL,
  decided_at    TEXT,
  decided_by    TEXT                    -- OS user of operator CLI
) STRICT;
```

Triggers (shape, one per append-only table): `CREATE TRIGGER run_event_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON run_event BEGIN SELECT
RAISE(ABORT,'append-only'); END;` and the same for `DELETE`. An analogous guard allows `approval` to change only from
`PENDING`. The controller never writes `EXPIRED` (no expiry: WF-7,
[ADR-0017](../adr/0017-approved-privileged-scope-bypass.md)); the value stays in the `CHECK` only so the M1 schema needs
no migration.

**Operator cancel requests (M1, Proposed).** `runs cancel` records a request as append-only `run_event` rows of type
`operator_cancel_requested` (payload `requested_by`, `reason`), later followed by `operator_cancel_applied`
(`request_event_id`, `state_after`, `applied_by`) or `operator_cancel_rejected` (`request_event_id`, `detail`). The daemon
and startup reconciliation apply pending requests. No table was added because `run_event` is already append-only and
transactional with state changes, the CLI works with no daemon, and a pending request is just a request event without a
matching applied or rejected event. This is Delegated, not owner-approved; it stays inside ADR-0005 (no new store).

**M9 additions (Proposed).** Project definitions live in the configuration file (operator) or in the ledger (projects an
orchestrator created, FR-PRJ-10) and are snapshotted into `run.policy_snapshot_json`; the ledger adds the project ID, one
append-only provenance table and one append-only project-revision table, in a forward migration (ADR-0005). `git_commit`
and `project_revision` join the append-only list above.

```sql
ALTER TABLE meta_session ADD COLUMN project_id TEXT;      -- NULL for runs delegated by workspace path (FR-PRJ-03)
ALTER TABLE run          ADD COLUMN project_id TEXT;

CREATE TABLE git_commit (               -- append-only provenance (FR-GIT-07); the commit itself carries none of this
  id              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                       -- prefix gcm_ (Proposed)
  commit_sha      TEXT NOT NULL,
  tree_sha        TEXT NOT NULL,
  patch_id        TEXT,                                   -- git patch-id of the commit; best-effort re-linking after rebase
  repo_root       TEXT NOT NULL,                          -- canonical context path
  publish_ref     TEXT NOT NULL,
  project_id      TEXT NOT NULL,
  context_id      TEXT NOT NULL,
  meta_session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES meta_session(id),
  run_id          TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES run(id),
  provider        TEXT NOT NULL,
  harness         TEXT NOT NULL,
  model           TEXT,
  adapter_version_id TEXT REFERENCES adapter_version(id),
  origin          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (origin IN ('commit_tool','finalize_only')),
  tool_event_id   TEXT REFERENCES run_event(id),          -- the git_commit run event
  sanitizer_json  TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(sanitizer_json)),   -- rule ids and counts only, never removed text
  created_at      TEXT NOT NULL
) STRICT;
CREATE INDEX git_commit_sha ON git_commit(commit_sha);
CREATE INDEX git_commit_run ON git_commit(run_id);

CREATE TABLE project_revision (         -- append-only; projects created over MCP (FR-PRJ-10). Current = highest revision
  id             TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                       -- prefix prv_ (Proposed)
  project_id     TEXT NOT NULL,                          -- never equal to a configured project ID
  revision       INTEGER NOT NULL,
  client_id      TEXT NOT NULL,                          -- creating MCP client; only it (and the operator) sees the project
  definition_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (json_valid(definition_json)),   -- same schema as a configured project (§11.1)
  status         TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE','PENDING_APPROVAL','DELETED')),
  approved_at    TEXT,                                   -- set by `projects approve`; NULL while PENDING_APPROVAL
  created_at     TEXT NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE (project_id, revision)
) STRICT;

CREATE TABLE git_push (                 -- append-only; one row per accepted or refused push (FR-GIT-05)
  id            TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  run_id        TEXT REFERENCES run(id),                  -- NULL for an operator-initiated push
  project_id    TEXT NOT NULL,
  context_id    TEXT NOT NULL,
  remote        TEXT NOT NULL,                            -- configured remote name, never a URL with credentials
  ref           TEXT NOT NULL,
  head_sha      TEXT NOT NULL,
  outcome       TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (outcome IN ('ACCEPTED','REFUSED_POLICY','REJECTED_REMOTE','ERROR')),
  detail        TEXT,                                     -- short, redacted
  requested_by  TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (requested_by IN ('agent_tool','operator')),
  created_at    TEXT NOT NULL
) STRICT;
```

New `run_event.type` values (M9): `environment` (create, start, stop, remove; M10), `git_commit`, `git_push`,
`git_policy_violation`, `git_tool_denied`. Payloads are bounded and hold no message text, only counts, SHAs and rule IDs.
Both new tables get the same `BEFORE UPDATE`/`DELETE` guards as the other append-only tables.

*As built (2026-10-13, Delegated, T9.2, [ADR-0024](../adr/0024-project-id-columns-and-project-snapshot.md)):* only the two
`ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN project_id TEXT` statements exist so far, as migration `0002_project_id`
(`src/ledger/migrations/0002_project_id.ts`; ledger schema version 2). No index and no constraint. `git_commit`,
`project_revision` and `git_push` come with T9.3 onward and T9.9. The run's `policy_snapshot_json` gains a `project`
object (`id`, `name`, `environment_kind`, `context_id`, `base_ref`, `effective_max_profile`, `allowed_providers?`;
`ProjectSnapshot` in `src/policy/types.ts`); push policy, identity and image digest are not in it yet.

*As built (2026-10-13, Delegated, T9.3, [ADR-0025](../adr/0025-git-commit-provenance-and-sanitiser.md)):* migration
`0003_git_commit` (`src/ledger/migrations/0003_git_commit.ts`; ledger schema version 3) creates `git_commit` and
`git_push` with the DDL above, the `BEFORE UPDATE`/`DELETE` guards (`git_commit_no_update`, `git_commit_no_delete`,
`git_push_no_update`, `git_push_no_delete`) and two additions: `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX git_commit_run_sha ON git_commit(run_id,
commit_sha)` (recovery re-inserts idempotently) and `CREATE INDEX git_push_run ON git_push(run_id)`. `git_push` has no
writer yet (T9.6); ID prefixes `gcm_` and `gps_` (the latter Proposed here). `Ledger.recordGitCommit` appends the
`git_commit` run event and inserts the row in one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction. New `run_event.type` values written by
T9.3: `git_commit_intent` and `git_commit_aborted` (see §12.3). `project_revision` is still not created (T9.9).

*As built (2026-10-13, Delegated, T9.5, [ADR-0027](../adr/0027-ref-snapshots-and-violation-handling.md)):* no migration.
New `run_event.type` values `git_ref_snapshot` and `git_ref_check`; `git_policy_violation` gets its first writer. Payloads
in [§12.5](#125-ref-snapshot-and-violation-handling).

*As built (2026-10-13, Delegated, T9.6, [ADR-0028](../adr/0028-controller-push-and-credential-reach.md)):* no migration.
`git_push` gets its writer, `Ledger.recordGitPush`, which appends the `git_push` run event and inserts the row in one
`BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction; `detail` holds a reason code from a fixed vocabulary (never Git output); `remote` and
`ref` are empty strings when an attempt was refused before a valid remote name or target existed; `run_id` is NULL for
an operator push, whose event goes on the run of the newest published commit. New `run_event.type` value
`git_push_withheld` (`{reason}`, BR-31). Payloads in [§12.6](#126-push-algorithm).

*As built (2026-10-13, Delegated, T9.9, [ADR-0030](../adr/0030-dynamic-project-revisions-and-approval.md)):* migration
`0004_project_revision` (`src/ledger/migrations/0004_project_revision.ts`; ledger schema version 4) creates
`project_revision` with the DDL above **except** `approved_at` (an append-only row cannot be updated) and **plus**
`approval_reasons_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'` (why a `PENDING_APPROVAL` revision waits), an `id` check for the
`prv_` prefix, `revision >= 1` and `CREATE INDEX project_revision_client ON project_revision(client_id, project_id)`.
Two more append-only tables: `project_revision_decision` (`prd_`; `project_id`, `revision`, `decision`
`APPROVED|REJECTED`, `decided_by`, `reason`, `decided_at`; unique `(project_id, revision)`, foreign key to the revision)
holds the operator's decision that LLD §11.2 called `approved_at`, and `project_event` (`pev_`; `ts`, `actor`
`mcp_client|operator`, `client_id`, `operator`, `action` `create_project|update_project|delete_project|approve|reject`,
`project_id`, `revision`, `outcome` `ACCEPTED|REFUSED`, `code` (set exactly when refused), `reason`, bounded
`payload_json`; index on `(project_id, ts)`) records every call, because `run_event.run_id` is `NOT NULL` and a project
call has no run. All three have `<table>_no_update` / `<table>_no_delete` guards. The store is `DynamicProjectStore`
(`src/projects/store.ts`); a project's state (in force, pending, approved, rejected, deleted) is derived from its rows.

**M11 additions (Proposed).** One forward migration (ADR-0005) adds the append-only `inner_call` table and the mutable
`inner_task_state` table (DDL in [§13.3](#133-data)), adds `pr_draft` to the `artifact.kind` check, adds `inner_agent` to
the `usage_observation.source` check (FR-INN-15), and adds two columns to `git_commit` (§12.7). T11.10 (2026-10-15,
Delegated) adds `handoff_condense` by migration 0016, the same rebuild, so the condensation sits beside its
`handoff_view`. SQLite cannot alter a
`CHECK`, so the two checks are widened by the migration runner's table-rebuild step. `route_decision.features_json` keeps
its column; only its content gains per-feature `source`, `model_id` and `template_version` (§13.6, `route_features`).
The inner agent's entities are therefore `inner_call` (history) and `inner_task_state` (current suspension state per
task), plus the columns above; no inner prompt or response body is stored anywhere (FR-INN-16).

**M7 and M12 additions (Proposed).** The M7 forward migration adds the mutable `project_affinity` table
([§3.2](#32-provider-and-model-affinity-m7), with the columns of [§17.6](#176-affinity-details-fills-32));
`route_decision` keeps its columns, `reasons_json` gains an `affinity` object and `features_json` a `schema_version`
([§17.4](#174-decision-record-fr-rte-03)). The M12 migration adds the append-only `rag_record` table
([§14.10](#1410-events-and-records)); the per-project and global retrieval stores are separate SQLite files outside
`ledger.db` ([§14.2](#142-stores-and-tables)). `rag_record` is added to the append-only list above.

**M5 additions (Proposed, Delegated, [ADR-0038](../adr/0038-handoff-bundle-and-continuity.md)).** No migration. The
`decision` table gets its first writer: `decision` holds a kind from a closed list, `reason_summary` a controller
template, `run_id` is always set, and no decision is written for a run after it is terminal
([§15.3](#153-decisions-and-open-work)). `meta_session.open_work_json` becomes a current-state projection of a pure
open-work derivation, rewritten when a run becomes terminal; nothing reads it as input. The `handoff_view` artifact kind
(migration 0009) holds the bundle of [§15.1](#151-bundle-schema-version-1). `provider_binding.harness`,
`backing_provider` and `model` are set at insert from `session_bound` (T5.3) and not updated afterwards.

## 2. Adapter contract

Satisfies FR-ADP-01 (probe, auth preflight, compile, parse, resume, cancel). Adapters are pure with respect to I/O
except `probe`/`authPreflight`, which execute the provider binary through a supervisor-provided `ProbeRunner` (so tests
inject fakes). Adapters never read credential files (FR-AUTH-01).

```ts
export type ProviderId = 'claude' | 'codex' | 'grok' | 'antigravity' | 'cursor' | 'opencode' | 'kimi' | 'muse' | 'fake';
export type TrustProfile = 'inspect-untrusted' | 'edit-untrusted' | 'test-sandboxed'
                         | 'trusted-development' | 'privileged';

export interface ProviderAdapter {
  readonly id: ProviderId;
  readonly transports: readonly Transport[];
  /** FR-GIT-01 declaration (M2, T2.2), reported by list_capabilities. */
  readonly gitWriteControl: GitWriteControl;
  /** M5 (T5.7): whether a native resume is tied to its working directory (§15.6). */
  readonly resumeCwd: 'bound' | 'independent' | 'unverified';

  /** FR-ADP-02 steps 1-5: binary, version, command, output formats, required flags. No auth, no model call. */
  probe(runner: ProbeRunner): Promise<ProbeResult>;
  /** Provider's own status command or a harmless headless call; never reads credentials (FR-AUTH-02). */
  authPreflight(runner: ProbeRunner): Promise<AuthResult>;
  /** Pure. Returns Ineligible (never a downgraded profile) when the profile cannot be satisfied (FR-POL-02). */
  compile(request: CompileRequest, profile: ResolvedProfile): Invocation | Ineligible;
  /** One stdout line (JSONL) or one ACP message -> zero or more events. Stateful via ParseState. */
  parseEvent(input: string | AcpMessage, state: ParseState): NormalizedEvent[];
  /** Folds the event stream plus exit info into the run result. */
  extractResult(events: readonly NormalizedEvent[], exit: ExitInfo): RunResult;
  /** Invocation fragment that continues a native session (FR-SES-03). Pure. */
  resumeArgs(binding: NativeBinding, request: CompileRequest, profile: ResolvedProfile): Invocation | Ineligible;
  /** Optional graceful cancel (e.g. ACP cancel message); supervisor still enforces SIGTERM/SIGKILL. */
  cancel?(handle: TransportHandle): Promise<void>;
  /** Maps evidence to one taxonomy code (section 4); unknown evidence -> OUTPUT_PROTOCOL_ERROR or TOOL_FAILURE, never success. */
  classifyFailure(evidence: FailureEvidence): FailureClassification;
}

export type Transport = 'subprocess-jsonl' | 'acp-stdio' | 'local-service'; // last deferred (FR-EXE-04)

/** FR-GIT-01 (Proposed shape, implemented in M2). */
export interface GitWriteControl {
  kind: 'provider-deny-rules'     // compile() passes the provider's documented deny controls for Git writes
      | 'none-documented';        // the provider documents none; FR-GIT-03 and FR-GIT-06 are what remains
  detail: string;                 // mechanism and source, or why none is documented; known limits
  remainingControls: string[];    // requirement IDs, e.g. ['FR-GIT-03', 'FR-GIT-06']
}

export interface ProbeResult {
  binaryPath: string; binaryVersion: string | null;
  steps: Array<{ step: 'binary'|'version'|'command'|'output_format'|'flags'|'smoke'; ok: boolean; detail?: string }>;
  modelsListed?: string[];              // feeds the model registry (FR-ADP-05)
}
export type AuthResult =
  | { state: 'OK' }
  | { state: 'AUTH_REQUIRED'; loginCommand: string }   // names the native command for the operator
  | { state: 'UNKNOWN'; reason: string };              // treated as ineligible (NFR-SEC-06)

export interface CompileRequest {
  runId: string; objective: string; constraints: string[];
  workspace: { cwd: string; writable: boolean };
  model?: string; verificationCommands?: string[][];
  handoff?: { bundleArtifactId: string };                // M5; ADR-0038 adds bundleSha256 (§15.5), record only
  bypassApproved?: boolean;  // T3.17, optional mirror of ResolvedProfile.bypassApproved (the carrier); adapters accept
                             // either, never compute it; the lifecycle sets the profile's value (§3.1 "Bypass")
}
export interface ResolvedProfile {                       // from COMP-POL, snapshot stored on the run
  name: TrustProfile; fileAccess: 'read-only'|'worktree'|'scoped';
  shell: 'none'|'allowlist'|'broad'|'scoped'; shellAllowlist?: string[];
  network: 'off'|'allowlist'|'policy'|'scoped'; networkAllowlist?: string[];
  repoConfig: 'disabled'|'approved-only'|'allowlisted'|'scoped';
  bypassApproved: boolean;                               // true only per §3.1 "Bypass" (FR-POL-03, ADR-0017)
  privilegedScope?: { scope: PrivilegedScope; scopeHash: string; approvalId: string };  // privileged only (§3.1)
}

export interface Invocation {
  transport: Transport;
  argv: string[];                       // argv[0] = absolute executable path from probe; never a shell string
  env: Record<string, string>;          // built from envAllowlist + adapter-declared vars only (FR-EXE-07)
  envAllowlist: string[];               // names, for audit and tests
  cwd: string;                          // run worktree (canonical)
  stdin?: { kind: 'text'; value: string } | { kind: 'none' };
  outputFormat: 'json' | 'jsonl' | 'acp';
  timeouts: { wallClockMs: number; idleMs: number; killGraceMs: number };
  policyFlags: string[];                // every security-relevant flag, for the bypass-flag audit
  expectedExitCodes: number[];
  maxLineBytes: number;
}
export interface Ineligible {
  ineligible: true;
  reason: 'PROFILE_UNSUPPORTED' | 'VERSION_UNVERIFIED' | 'NO_MANIFEST' | 'AUTH_REQUIRED' | 'CAPABILITY_MISSING';
  detail: string;                       // surfaced via explain_route and FR-MCP-05
}
```

**Handoff and resume (M5, Proposed, Delegated, [ADR-0038](../adr/0038-handoff-bundle-and-continuity.md)).** No
adapter renders a bundle: for a handoff run the lifecycle puts the provider-neutral rendering of
[§15.5](#155-rendering) in `objective`, passes `constraints: []` and sets `handoff` for the record; adapters never read
the artifact. Each adapter gains the static declaration `resumeCwd: 'bound' | 'independent' | 'unverified'` (T5.7);
`resumeArgs` is unchanged, and the lifecycle chooses the resume's working directory by [§15.6](#156-working-directory-of-a-native-resume).

**Provider ids.** Two id spaces exist (Proposed, M2). Config files and `providers.<id>` use the provider names from the
roadmap; the adapter contract, the ledger and MCP tools use the `ProviderId` above. *As built (T4.1, 2026-10-13,
Delegated, [ADR-0035](../adr/0035-provider-registry-table-and-declared-bypass-flags.md)):* both come from one provider
registry table, `BUILTIN_PROVIDERS` in `src/adapters/providers.ts`; `ProviderId` in `src/adapters/types.ts`,
`PROVIDER_IDS` and the `providers` and `allowed_providers` schema parts in `src/config/schema.ts`,
`CONFIG_TO_ADAPTER_ID` in `src/routing/collect.ts` and the `serve` and `doctor` registrations are derived from it.

| Config provider id | Adapter `ProviderId` | In the table (T4.1) | Declared bypass-class flags (source) | Declared short flags and values (ADR-0037; source) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `claude-code` | `claude` | Adapter | `--dangerously-skip-permissions`, `--allow-dangerously-skip-permissions` ([TDD §4.1.1](TDD.md#411-claude-code-native-controls-per-profile-and-platform-t30), `claude --help` 2.1.296) | Value `--permission-mode bypassPermissions` (same source); active |
| `codex` | `codex` | Adapter | `--yolo`, `--full-auto` ([R2] L28, L125, L126) | None |
| `grok-build` | `grok` | Module `src/adapters/grok/` (T4.22; manifest range admits no version) | None (its long bypass flags are shared `BYPASS_FLAGS`) | Values `--permission-mode bypassPermissions`; `--allow` and `--allowed-tools` with `*` or `**` (catch-all, Proposed) ([TDD §4.3.1](TDD.md#431-grok-build-native-controls-per-profile-and-platform-t420)); active since T4.22 |
| `antigravity` | `antigravity` | Module `src/adapters/antigravity/` (T4.32; manifest range admits no version) | `-dangerously-skip-permissions` (the two-dash form is shared), `--remote-control`, `-remote-control`, `--gemini_dir`, `-gemini_dir`, `--add-dir`, `-add-dir` ([TDD §4.4](TDD.md#44-antigravity-agy-m4), the T4.31 live check, [ADR-0045](../adr/0045-one-dash-long-bypass-flag-spellings.md); Proposed) | None ([TDD §4.4.2](TDD.md#442-antigravity-native-controls-per-profile-and-platform-t430): `always-proceed` is a settings value) |
| `cursor` | `cursor` | Module `src/adapters/cursor/` (T4.42; manifest range admits no version) | `--auto-review`, `--worktree`, `--worktree-base`, `--skip-worktree-setup`, `--workspace`, `--add-dir`, `--plugin-dir`, `--api-key`, `--auth-token`, `--header`, `--endpoint` (`--force`, `--yolo`, `--approve-mcps` are shared; [TDD §4.5](TDD.md#45-cursor-cli-m4) and the T4.41 `--help`, Proposed) | Short `-f`, `-w`, `-H`, `-e`; value `--sandbox disabled` ([TDD §4.5](TDD.md#45-cursor-cli-m4), Proposed); active since T4.42 |
| `opencode` | `opencode` | Module `src/adapters/opencode/` (T4.52; manifest range admits no version) | `--auto` ([TDD §4.6.2](TDD.md#462-inventory-and-decisions-for-later-tasks-t450), Proposed; never emitted, Q-83) | None (the inline `allow` permission is carried in the environment, which the adapter never sets, [TDD §4.6.2](TDD.md#462-inventory-and-decisions-for-later-tasks-t450)) |
| `kimi-code` | `kimi` | Module `src/adapters/kimi/` (T4.62; manifest range admits no version) | `--auto`, `--plan` (`--yolo` is shared; [ADR-0049](../adr/0049-kimi-code-transport-and-bypass-class.md), [TDD §4.7](TDD.md#47-kimi-code-m4-blocked-by-b-1)). `-p`/`--prompt` are bypass-class (ADR-0049) but not declared: the audit is shared and Claude Code `-p` and Antigravity `--prompt` are ordinary flags (Q-95); the compile gate alone emits `--prompt` | Short `-y`; ACP modes `auto`, `yolo` and the `mode` option with those values (ADR-0049 item 2); active since T4.62 |
| `muse-code` | `muse` | Not yet: added with its adapter module (T4.72), as the `ProviderId` above anticipates | None yet (T4.70) | Values `--approval-mode never`, `--sandbox-network unrestricted` ([TDD §4.8](TDD.md#48-muse-code-m4), Proposed), declared when the entry is added |
| none | `fake` | Test-only adapter (`serve` registers it only behind the test-only switch) | None | None |

A reserved entry is accepted in configuration and never registered. The argv audit refuses the shared `BYPASS_FLAGS`
plus every flag the table declares, except in the approved bypass case (FR-POL-03, ADR-0017, ADR-0035); a declaration
can only add to the shared list.

*Audit contract (T4.1 audit gaps, 2026-10-13, Delegated, [ADR-0037](../adr/0037-argv-audit-short-flags-and-bypass-values.md)):*
before every spawn `findBypassFlags` reads `argv`, then `policyFlags`, word by word, and reports each hit once:

| Form | Matches | Reported as |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Flag (`BYPASS_FLAGS` and every entry's `bypassFlags`) | The word equals the flag or starts with `<flag>=` (unchanged since T3.17) | The flag |
| Short flag (`bypassShortFlags` of entries with `create`, and any `-x` in the flag list) | A word of one dash and letters or digits, with an optional `=value`, whose letters include the short flag's letter (`-f`, `-f=1`, `-pf`): deliberate over-refusal | The short flag |
| Value (`bypassValues` of entries with `create`) | The flag word directly followed by the value word, or `<flag>=<value>`, within one list; value compared case-insensitively | `<flag> <value>` |

Any hit is refused (`BypassFlagError`) unless the run's context is `privileged` with `bypassApproved` and a scope that
sets `bypass`. Registration refuses malformed declarations: short flags must be `-x`; values must be
`{ flag, value }` with a flag of the `bypassFlags` shape and a value of 1 to 128 characters without whitespace or
control characters. Settings carried in the child environment or in files are not seen (ADR-0037 "Limits").

**Ineligible to failure code.** The adapter reasons map to the codes in §4 per
[FRD BR-3](../requirements/FRD.md#3-business-rules): `AUTH_REQUIRED` gives `AUTH_REQUIRED`; `VERSION_UNVERIFIED` gives
`ADAPTER_VERSION_UNVERIFIED`; `NO_MANIFEST`, `PROFILE_UNSUPPORTED` and `CAPABILITY_MISSING` give `ELIGIBILITY_NONE`.

`NormalizedEvent` (observable facts only, NFR-PRIV-02; no provider reasoning text):

```ts
export type NormalizedEvent =
  | { kind: 'session_bound'; nativeSessionId: string; model?: string; backingProvider?: string }
  | { kind: 'tool_call'; tool: 'read'|'edit'|'shell'|'other'; name: string; target?: string; denied?: boolean }
  | { kind: 'message'; role: 'assistant'; text: string }               // bounded; final-visible text only
  | { kind: 'usage'; source: 'provider_reported'; metrics: Record<string, number|string|null> }
  | { kind: 'rate_limit'; resetAt?: string; reportedCategory?: string }
  | { kind: 'permission_denied'; tool: string }                         // -> PERMISSION_BLOCKED
  | { kind: 'sandbox_violation'; detail: string }                       // -> SANDBOX_VIOLATION
  | { kind: 'provider_error'; category: string; message: string }
  | { kind: 'result'; ok: boolean; summary?: string }
  | { kind: 'unknown'; rawType: string };                               // counted, recorded, never trusted
```

Parsing rules: unparseable framing yields no event and the supervisor ends the run with `OUTPUT_PROTOCOL_ERROR`
(NFR-SEC-06). Fields are taken from structured JSON only, never regex over free text (FR-ADP-04). `FailureEvidence`
bundles `{events, exit, stderrTail (bounded), timedOut, cancelled}`. A central bypass-flag audit runs over
`Invocation.policyFlags` and `argv` before spawn (FR-POL-03). Per-adapter parsing specifics: to be detailed in M2
(Claude) and M4 (others).

**Harness and backing provider (M4, T4.6, FR-ADP-10; Delegated).** An adapter whose harness fronts several vendors
sets `backingProvider` (and `model`) on `session_bound`, from structured output only; it never derives a vendor from a
display name (TDD §4.5), and omits the field when the harness does not report it. `usageObservationsFromEvents`
(`src/usage/observations.ts`) copies the latest `session_bound` `model` and `backingProvider` onto every
`usage_observation` row of the run, with `harness` the adapter id; no new column or table. Shown as follows:
`get_run` `usage.reported[]` carries `harness`, `backing_provider` (null when not reported) and `model`; `runs show`
adds a top-level `usage` array (same fields plus `observed_at` and `metrics`; absent when the run has no
provider-reported row) and prints one `harness H  backing provider B  model M` line per row. Capacity keys stay with M6.

**Transport kinds and the ACP plan (M4, Proposed, Delegated, T4.7,
[ADR-0036](../adr/0036-per-run-acp-stdio-client.md)).** `subprocess-jsonl` (M1) and `acp-stdio` (M4, FR-EXE-03) are
both one provider process per run; `local-service` stays deferred (FR-EXE-04). For `acp-stdio`, `compile` and
`resumeArgs` return an `Invocation` with `transport: 'acp-stdio'`, `outputFormat: 'acp'`, the agent's argv (for example
`grok agent stdio`, [TDD §4.3.1](TDD.md#431-grok-build-native-controls-per-profile-and-platform-t420)), no `stdin` text
(the client owns stdin) and an `acp` plan. The shared client in `src/exec/acp/` speaks JSON-RPC; the adapter never
does. Method, field and variant names below are from the ACP specification v1 (agentclientprotocol.com, retrieved
2026-10-13; sources in ADR-0036); provider behaviour behind them is Unverified until each provider's operator capture.

```ts
export interface AcpPlan {                        // pure data: Invocation.acp when transport is 'acp-stdio'
  protocolVersion: 1;                             // v2 is draft; any other answer -> OUTPUT_PROTOCOL_ERROR
  prompt: string;                                 // sent as one `text` content block in `session/prompt`
  session: { kind: 'new' }                        // `session/new`, cwd = Invocation.cwd
         | { kind: 'resume'; nativeSessionId: string };  // `session/resume` if advertised, else `session/load`
  mcpServers: AcpStdioServer[];                   // [] unless ADR-0036 item 7.3 holds (shim only)
  modeId?: string;                                // `session/set_mode` value compiled for the profile, audited
  configOptions?: Array<{ configId: string; value: string }>;  // `session/set_config_option`, audited (Unverified use)
  loginCommand: string;                           // native login named on AUTH_REQUIRED (FR-AUTH-02)
}
export interface AcpStdioServer { name: string; command: string; args: string[]; env: [] }  // token never here
export interface AcpDeclarations {                // on the provider registry entry, additive only (ADR-0035)
  bypassModeIds: string[];                        // refused outside the approved bypass case (FR-POL-03)
  bypassConfigValues: Array<{ configId: string; value: string }>;
  /** Optional parsers from an agent-reported tool call to an argv or a host; used only to narrow (ADR-0036 9.2). */
  parseExecute?(toolCall: unknown): string[] | null;
  parseFetchHost?(toolCall: unknown): string | null;
}
export interface AcpPermissionSnapshot {          // from the frozen policy snapshot only
  profile: ResolvedProfile; worktree: string; readOnlyContexts: string[];
}

/** Pure; COMP-POL (`src/policy/acp-permission.ts`). Inputs: the snapshot and the request only. Never throws. */
export function decidePermission(request: AcpMessage, snapshot: AcpPermissionSnapshot,
                                 decl: AcpDeclarations): AcpPermissionDecision;
export type AcpPermissionDecision =
  | { answer: 'allow_once'; optionId: string; reason: string }
  | { answer: 'reject_once'; optionId: string; reason: string }
  | { answer: 'cancel_turn'; reason: 'no_reject_option' | 'run_cancelling' };  // outcome `cancelled`
```

Rules (ADR-0036 items 6 to 11): the client never sends `authenticate`, `logout` or a `terminal` auth method;
`clientCapabilities` advertise `fs` and `terminal` as `false` and nothing else, so `fs/*`, `terminal/*`,
`elicitation/create`, `_`-prefixed and provider-specific methods get `-32601`; `decidePermission` denies by default,
selects only `allow_once` or `reject_once`, never `allow_always` or `reject_always`; an allow-everything rule is
bypass-class and reachable only in an approved bypass scope (open for zemdregon, ADR-0036 open item 1; until then an
approved bypass run on `acp-stdio` is `Ineligible: PROFILE_UNSUPPORTED`). The pre-spawn audit also refuses an `acp`
plan whose `modeId` or `configOptions` match the registry's declared bypass values outside that case, or whose
`mcpServers` is not empty without ADR-0036 item 7.3.
*Note 2026-10-13 (owner decision 48; the sentence above stays as written):* open item 1 is answered, so in an approved
bypass run `decidePermission` allows every request with `allow_once` (never `allow_always`), and an adapter may compile
that run on `acp-stdio`.

*As built (2026-10-13, Delegated, T4.8; not owner-approved).* `AcpPlan`, `AcpStdioServer` and `AcpDeclarations` are in
`src/adapters/types.ts` (`Invocation.acp`); a registry entry declares `acp` (`src/adapters/providers.ts`; the fake entry
declares a made-up mode and option for tests). `decidePermission(request, snapshot, decl?)` is in
`src/policy/acp-permission.ts`; `request` is the `session/request_permission` params, and the snapshot gains
`writable` (edits need a writable workspace). A privileged scope's `files`, `shell` (exact argv entries) and `network`
(`on` or host list) are read in place of the profile's `scoped` fields; a profile shell allowlist entry is split on
whitespace into an argv. The decision is `cancel_turn` with the denial reason when no `reject_once` option exists. It
resolves symlinks in the longest existing prefix of each location (`realpathSync`), the one file-system read item 9.2
needs. `auditAcpPlan` (`src/adapters/audit.ts`) refuses every non-empty `mcpServers`, because no per-provider ADR
accepts an ACP channel yet (fail closed); declared bypass mode ids and option values are compared case-insensitively.
The shared mapping is `mapAcpMessage` (`src/exec/acp/events.ts`): message chunks are joined per `messageId` up to 4,000
characters (the Claude Code parser's bound) and flushed by any other update or the result; `tool_call` `target` is the
first location; `usage_update` `cost` as an object becomes `cost_amount` and `cost_currency`; `current_mode_update` not
caught by the client is `unknown`. The run events are written by the lifecycle through `run_event` (no migration):
`acp_initialized` (`protocol_version`, boolean capability flags only, auth method `id` and `type`), `acp_permission`
(`tool_kind`, `decision`, `reason`, `option_kind`, `location_count`; after 256, one `{summary, suppressed}` event) and
`acp_request_refused` (`method`).

`parseEvent` receives the `session/update` notifications and the final `session/prompt` response as `AcpMessage`s.
Mapping to `NormalizedEvent`:

| ACP input | `NormalizedEvent` |
| --- | --- |
| `session/new` result `sessionId` (passed on by the client) | `session_bound` |
| `agent_message_chunk` (joined per `messageId`, bounded) | `message` |
| `agent_thought_chunk` | none (NFR-PRIV-02) |
| `user_message_chunk`; updates replayed by `session/load` | none (counted) |
| `tool_call`, `tool_call_update` | `tool_call`: `read`/`search` to `read`; `edit`/`delete`/`move` to `edit`; `execute` to `shell`; else `other`; `denied` when the client rejected it |
| Client rejected a permission request | `permission_denied` (then `PERMISSION_BLOCKED`) |
| `usage_update` (`used`, `size`, `cost`) | `usage` (`provider_reported`) |
| `current_mode_update` to a declared bypass mode outside the bypass case | `permission_denied` (detail `acp_bypass_mode`; the client cancels) |
| `plan`, `available_commands_update`, `config_option_update`, `session_info_update`, any other variant | `unknown` |
| Prompt result `stopReason: end_turn` | `result` `ok: true` |
| `max_tokens`, `max_turn_requests`, `refusal`; `cancelled` not requested by the controller | `result` `ok: false` (`TOOL_FAILURE` unless the adapter maps it, Proposed) |
| `cancelled` after a controller cancel | run `CANCELLED` |
| Error response `-32000` on any request | `AUTH_REQUIRED` |
| Other error response | `provider_error` with the code as `category` |

New run event types (ADR-0036): `acp_initialized` (agent capabilities, auth method ids and types only),
`acp_permission` (tool kind, decision, reason code, option kind, location count; at most 256 per run, then counted),
`acp_request_refused` (method name). The `cancel` hook of an `acp-stdio` adapter is the shared client's cancel sequence
([§8](#8-run-supervisor-details)).

**M9 additions to the adapter contract (Proposed).** Each adapter also declares how it meets FR-GIT-02. (The FR-GIT-01
part, first drafted here as `gitWrites: 'denied-by-provider-rules' | 'no-shell-profiles-only' | 'undeclared'`, was
implemented in M2 as `gitWriteControl` above; `undeclared` became `none-documented` because FR-GIT-01 requires the
adapter to declare the absence.)

```ts
export interface GitCapabilities {
  /** FR-GIT-02: can the per-run injected tool channel be supplied without a bypass flag (FR-POL-03)? */
  commitChannel: 'injected' | 'finalize-only' | 'none';
  /** Pure: the MCP definition for the shim, in the provider's own per-run form; never written to global config (FR-POL-08). */
  channelArgs?(shim: ShimSpec, request: CompileRequest, profile: ResolvedProfile): Pick<Invocation, 'argv' | 'env'> | Ineligible;
}
export interface ShimSpec { command: string[]; socketPath: string; toolNames: ('commit' | 'push')[]; }
```

`compile` appends the deny rules and, for `commitChannel: 'injected'`, the `channelArgs` result. An adapter with
`commitChannel: 'none'` is ineligible for write-capable profiles on a context unless the operator opts in to
finalize-only mode (BR-30). The bypass-flag audit (FR-POL-03) treats any flag whose only purpose is approving the
injected server as a bypass flag. Which providers fall in which category is **to be verified** per adapter
([TDD §3.10](TDD.md#310-git-commits-attribution-and-publication)).

**As built (2026-10-13, Delegated, T9.4, ADR-0026).** `GitCapabilities` and `ShimSpec` are in `src/adapters/types.ts`
with two additions: `GitCapabilities.detail` (the reason for the declaration) and `ShimSpec.env` (the token variable for
the shim). `ShimSpec.command` is the full argv including `--socket <path>`. The lifecycle, not `compile`, appends the
`channelArgs` result (above). Fake: `injected`; Claude Code and Codex: `none`. The rule that a `none` adapter is
ineligible for write-capable profiles without the finalize-only opt-in is not enforced yet (ROADMAP Q-43).

*Note 2026-10-13 (owner decision 36, Q-43; the sentence in the paragraph above stays as written).* zemdregon chose
finalize-only ("Finalize-only."): an adapter with `commitChannel: 'none'` is **not** made ineligible for write-capable
profiles, so that rule is not to be enforced. Such an adapter runs write-capable runs and, when the project opts in to
finalize-only mode (T9.7, BR-30), the controller makes the commit at FINALIZING through the same sanitiser and
provenance as the commit tool. zemdregon wants the opt-in enabled for his own projects. Claude Code's `commitChannel` may
become `injected` behind an operator opt-in once T9.4b and its live checks pass ([ADR-0029](../adr/0029-claude-code-receives-tool-shim-via-mcp-config.md)).

### 2.0 Antigravity adapter implementation evidence (M4 T4.32, T4.33, T4.37)

Compile matrix, transport and declared flags: [TDD §4.4](TDD.md#44-antigravity-agy-m4). Task status and remaining
gaps: [ROADMAP §6.8](../ROADMAP.md#68-m4-adapter-expansion-tasks) (`T4.32`, `T4.33`, `T4.37`). On `origin/main` as of
[#208](https://github.com/zemdregon/switchboard/pull/208) (2026-10-14, Delegated, not owner-approved; synthetic
fixtures only; no `agy` in CI).

**As built (T4.32).** Module `src/adapters/antigravity/` (`adapter.ts`, `protocol.ts`; spellings cite TDD §4.4 or the
T4.31 live check, with `--prompt=` marked Assumption where unobserved). Manifest `src/adapters/manifests/antigravity.json`
(range `<0.0.0` until `adapters validate` passes a live CLI). One registry entry with `create` and bypass-class flags in
`src/adapters/providers.ts` ([ADR-0045](../adr/0045-one-dash-long-bypass-flag-spellings.md)). Transport
`subprocess-jsonl` with `outputFormat` `jsonl`. Every non-`privileged` profile and every `privileged` scope without an
approved bypass is `Ineligible: PROFILE_UNSUPPORTED` because `toolPermission` is a settings value, not per run
([TDD §4.4.2](TDD.md#442-antigravity-native-controls-per-profile-and-platform-t430), Q-63). The sole compiled shape is
the Q-84 bypass cell: `--prompt=<objective>`, `--output-format stream-json`, `--dangerously-skip-permissions`, env
`AGY_CLI_DISABLE_AUTO_UPDATE=true` only (no API-key variables). Static declarations on the adapter: `gitWriteControl`
`none-documented`, `commitChannel` `finalize-only`, `resumeCwd` `bound`.

- **Tests:** `src/adapters/antigravity/antigravity.test.ts` (`antigravity compile matrix (T4.32)`,
  `antigravity bypass-class declarations (ADR-0035, ADR-0045)`, `antigravity probe and auth placeholder (fail closed
  until T4.34)`, `antigravity registry entry and manifest gate (T4.32)`); `src/adapters/providers.test.ts` (declared
  bypass spellings).

**As built (T4.33, Partial).** `parseEvent`, `extractResult`, `classifyFailure` and `resumeArgs` in `adapter.ts`; event
names and status prefixes in `protocol.ts`. Structured JSONL only (FR-ADP-04): `session_bound` from `init`, never from
free text. Success needs a clean expected exit, a `result` with `status` `SUCCESS`, run-total `total_tokens` above zero,
and no `denied_actions` or `error` text (Q-86); envelope usage is emitted once as `provider_reported` (a second `result`
line records no second `usage` row, [#227](https://github.com/zemdregon/switchboard/pull/227)). A soft-denied tool
(`denied_actions`) ends `PERMISSION_BLOCKED` even when `status` is `SUCCESS` and the exit is 0. A `step_update` whose
`conversation_id` differs from the bound id ends `OUTPUT_PROTOCOL_ERROR` while its tool call still reaches the guard; a
step never binds the session. Invalid model and failed login map to `MODEL_UNAVAILABLE` and `AUTH_REQUIRED`; malformed
and unknown lines become `unknown` events. `extractResult` precedence: cancel and timeout over a denial, denial over
envelope error and non-zero exit, ADR-0039 guard over all ([#227](https://github.com/zemdregon/switchboard/pull/227)).
Resume is not compiled: `resumeArgs` is always `Ineligible` and `--conversation` never appears in argv (Q-86). Shapes
that stayed on stderr in the live check (for example `AGY_ERROR`) are outside `FailureEvidence` here, so those runs end
`TOOL_FAILURE` until a capture shows them on the stdout stream the supervisor parses.

- **Tests:** `src/adapters/antigravity/antigravity.test.ts` (`antigravity parser and result (T4.33)`,
  `antigravity parser hardening (T4.33, synthetic shapes)`).
- **Design sourcing:** [TDD §4.4.7](TDD.md#447-antigravity-parser-implementation-evidence-m4-t433); HLD [§2.2
  T4.33](HLD.md#t433--parser-result-failure-classification-and-resume-partial).

**As built (T4.37).** Git declarations and Done-when for FR-GIT-01, FR-GIT-02 and FR-GIT-09
([TDD §4.4](TDD.md#44-antigravity-agy-m4) "Git writes and injected tools",
[ADR-0039](../adr/0039-switchboard-side-tool-call-guard.md), owner decision 36). Lands in
[#224](https://github.com/zemdregon/switchboard/pull/224) on `sb/2026-10-14-t437-antigravity-git` (2026-10-14, Delegated;
synthetic and replay fixtures only; no `agy` in CI).

- **Static declarations (FR-GIT-01, FR-GIT-02).** `ANTIGRAVITY_GIT_WRITE_CONTROL` and `ANTIGRAVITY_GIT_CAPABILITIES` on
  `AntigravityAdapter`; `GIT_WRITE_CONTROL_DETAIL` in `protocol.ts`. `kind` `none-documented`, `remainingControls`
  `["FR-GIT-03", "FR-GIT-06"]`; `commitChannel` `finalize-only` with detail that MCP servers come only from global and
  workspace `mcp_config.json` and need a settings allow rule, so no tool channel is injected (FR-GIT-09).
- **Compile guard (FR-GIT-01).** Check 0 in `#build`: `gitWriteRisks` over `profile.shellAllowlist` rejects entries that
  could write Git as `PROFILE_UNSUPPORTED` citing FR-GIT-01 on every profile, including an approved bypass scope. Read-only
  `git status` / `git diff` allowlist entries do not trigger that refusal (non-`privileged` profiles stay ineligible for
  other reasons).
- **Argv and env (FR-GIT-02).** The compiled shape keeps the objective in one `--prompt=` word; no `git` or `gh` in argv
  or `policyFlags`; `envAllowlist` is `PATH` and `HOME` only; child `env` carries no Git or provider credential keys.
- **MCP surface.** `list_capabilities` reports `git_write_control` matching the adapter constants.
- **Finalize-only (FR-GIT-09).** A replayed successful edit (`success_edit` fixture) in a project whose primary context
  has `finalize_commit: true` receives one controller commit at FINALIZING (T9.7; same path as other finalize-only
  adapters).
- **Tests:** `src/adapters/antigravity/antigravity.test.ts` (`antigravity Git declaration (T4.37, FR-GIT-01, FR-GIT-02,
  FR-GIT-09)`): declarations, `list_capabilities`, argv and allowlist guards, finalize-only lifecycle leg.

**Not covered by this evidence (their own tasks):** auth preflight beyond the `UNKNOWN` placeholder (T4.34), conformance
suite (T4.35), malicious-fixture leg (T4.36), operator live smoke and `adapters validate` (T4.38).

### 2.1 Model registry (M4, T4.5)

Satisfies FR-ADP-05: model identities, aliases and context limits come from a refreshable registry, never from adapter
source. **Delegated** (not owner-approved); every key, pattern and default below is a Proposed shape. No ledger table
and no ADR: the registry is configuration plus an in-memory refresh, so a restart rebuilds it from the file and the next
probe.

**Configuration (`providers.<id>.models`, strict keys).** Replaces the earlier TDD §3.1 placeholder
`model_overrides`. `<id>` is a config provider id.

```jsonc
"providers": { "<id>": { "models": {
  "ids": ["<model-id>"],                    // operator-held model ids; at most 256, unique
  "aliases": { "<alias>": "<model-id>" },   // alias to the id passed to the provider; at most 256
  "context_limits": { "<model-id>": 0 },    // positive integer tokens, as the operator states them
  "capabilities": { "<model-id>": "low" }   // T7.8: "low" | "medium" | "high", operator-declared
} } }
```

An id, alias or key matches `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:/@+-]{0,127}$` (**Assumption**: plain-identifier shape, which also
keeps option-like values out of argv). Examples use placeholders: neither this document nor adapter source names a model.

**In-memory state (`src/models/registry.ts`, `ModelRegistry`).** Per adapter id: the configured `ids`, `aliases`,
`context_limits` and `capabilities`, and the last *listing*, a set of ids taken from `ProbeResult.modelsListed`.

**Refresh rule.** Whenever eligibility collects facts and a provider's probe completed with every step `ok`, the
collector calls `refresh(adapterId, probe.modelsListed)`:

- `modelsListed` absent: the previous listing is kept (an adapter that cannot list never clears the registry; a probe
  that fails any step does not reach this call).
- `modelsListed` present: it **replaces** the listing for that provider (an empty array clears it), so a model the
  provider stopped listing leaves the registry at the next probe and a new one enters, with no code change. Entries that
  do not match the id pattern are dropped.
- Cadence is that of the probe, so the preflight cache TTL (`timeouts.preflight_ttl_seconds`, NFR-PERF-02) bounds the
  staleness; a restart starts with no listing until the first probe.
- Adapters supply the listing from the provider's documented listing command through the `ProbeRunner`; none stores a
  name. An adapter that has no listing command leaves `modelsListed` unset.

**Check rule.** A request's `model` (`CapabilityRequirements.model`, then `CompileRequest.model`) is resolved per
provider before the adapter's `compile` is asked:

1. A provider is *restricted* when its configured `ids`, the targets of its `aliases` or its current listing hold at
   least one id. Otherwise it is *unrestricted*.
2. Unrestricted: the model passes through unchanged (**Delegated default, backward compatible**: without registry data
   the behaviour before T4.5 holds, and the adapter's own `compile` still decides; `context_limits` alone does not
   restrict).
3. Restricted: an alias resolves to its target id; an id held by `ids` or the listing passes; anything else is
   `Ineligible` with reason `CAPABILITY_MISSING` and a detail naming the provider, the requested value and the reason
   (not configured, not listed). The detail never echoes more than 80 characters of the request.
4. The resolved id, not the alias, goes into `CompileRequest.model`.

The `Ineligible` follows the usual BR-3 mapping (`ELIGIBILITY_NONE`, or the named provider's reason under
`require_provider`). `contextLimit(provider, id)` returns the configured limit or nothing; no consumer exists before M6/M7
(capacity and affinity), and no default limit is ever supplied by Switchboard.

**Capability floor and `minimum_capability` routing (T7.8, FR-RTE-01).** When a request specifies `minimum_capability`
(`low`, `medium`, or `high`) in `routing`:

1. Capability order is strictly `low` < `medium` < `high`. A model declared at capability $C$ meets floor $F$ if and only
   if $\text{rank}(C) \ge \text{rank}(F)$.
2. Operator-declared model capability comes from `providers.<id>.models.capabilities`. Switchboard assumes no capability
   for any real model.
3. Undeclared models fail closed with `CAPABILITY_MISSING`. An unrestricted provider (no `models` configuration) or a
   provider with no models declared at or above the requested floor is ineligible (`CAPABILITY_MISSING`, detail naming the
   provider and floor).
4. Candidate models for an eligible provider are filtered to those meeting the floor; each candidate is verified against
   the adapter's `compileCheck` before admission. With T7.12 model pinning, admitted providers compile with the candidate
   model that satisfied the capability floor (`facts.model`), recorded in `request_json.model` and decision records.
5. When `require_provider` is true, an ineligible required provider returns the BR-3 reason code (`ELIGIBILITY_NONE` with
   `"required provider <provider> is ineligible: CAPABILITY_MISSING"`). When no providers meet the floor, routing returns
   `ELIGIBILITY_NONE` with per-provider exclusion reasons.

**Hard constraint mapping onto existing reasons (T7.8, FR-RTE-01, BR-3).** FR-RTE-01 requires evaluating the hard
constraints and mapping exclusions onto existing reason codes:

- *Minimum capability:* evaluated against operator-declared registry capabilities; models below the floor or
  undeclared fail closed with `CAPABILITY_MISSING` (mapping to `ELIGIBILITY_NONE` under BR-3 or the required
  provider reason).
- *Sandbox strength:* enforced via trust profile compilation (`PROFILE_UNSUPPORTED`) and platform verification
  (`PROFILE_PLATFORM_UNVERIFIED`).
- *Required tools:* enforced via adapter capability check and trust profile compilation (`CAPABILITY_MISSING` /
  `PROFILE_UNSUPPORTED`).
- *Concurrency (Q-5: queue):* providers at concurrency limit or ingress queue full map to `ELIGIBILITY_NONE` with
  reason `QUEUE_FULL` (BR-3 "at concurrency", ROADMAP Q-5).
- *Health:* mapped to circuit breaker cool-down (`CIRCUIT_OPEN`) and binary/probe failure (`PROBE_FAILED`), both
  mapping to `ELIGIBILITY_NONE` under BR-3.

**Dispatch compile and model wiring (T7.12, T7.8).** Dispatch-time `compile` accepts a `model` parameter; admitted
providers compile with the model that satisfied `minimum_capability` (T7.8) or the remembered model under affinity model
pinning (T7.12). `list_capabilities` `models` reflects the registry listings.

## 3. MCP tool contracts

Schemas are published JSON Schema (draft 2020-12; version choice Proposed) validated with Ajv before any side effect
(FR-MCP-09). Shown abbreviated; all objects have `additionalProperties: false`. Tools are async-first: `delegate_task`
returns immediately unless `wait_seconds` is given ([ADR-0002](../adr/0002-mcp-upstream-acp-downstream.md), FR-MCP-03).

### 3.0 Shared types

```jsonc
{ "$defs": {
  "trust_profile": { "enum": ["inspect-untrusted","edit-untrusted","test-sandboxed","trusted-development","privileged"] },
  "routing": { "type": "object", "properties": {
      "provider_preference": { "type": "string" },
      "require_provider": { "type": "boolean", "default": false },
      "minimum_capability": { "enum": ["low","medium","high"] } } },
  "status": { "enum": ["AWAITING_APPROVAL","QUEUED","WAITING_CAPACITY","PREFLIGHT","LEASED","RUNNING",
                       "VERIFYING","FINALIZING","SUCCEEDED","FAILED","CANCELLED"] },
  "envelope": { "type": "object", "required": ["run_id","meta_session_id","status"], "properties": {
      "run_id": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^run_[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26}$" },
      "meta_session_id": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^sess_[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26}$" },
      "status": { "$ref": "#/$defs/status" },
      "failure_code": { "type": "string" },
      "provider": { "type": "object", "properties": { "adapter": {}, "model": {}, "adapter_version": {} } },
      "route": { "type": "object", "properties": { "reasons": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } } } },
      "result": { "type": "object", "properties": { "summary": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 4000 },
          "changed_files": { "type": "integer" },
          "verification": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "object" } },   // per command, §8.1
          "verification_summary": { "type": "object" } } },  // M11, T11.9: display-only prose, see §13.6
      "artifacts": { "type": "object", "additionalProperties": { "type": "string" } },   // kind -> art_ id
      "approval": { "type": "object", "required": ["status","requested_at"], "properties": {   // privileged only (M3, §3.1)
          "status": { "enum": ["PENDING","APPROVED","DENIED"] }, "requested_at": { "type": "string" } } },
      "usage": { "type": "object", "properties": { "reported": {}, "estimated": {}, "capacity_confidence": { "type": ["number","null"] } } } } } } }
```

The envelope never contains raw transcripts or credential material (FR-MCP-04); `provider_stdout`/`provider_stderr`
artifacts are operator-CLI only.

**`delegate_task`** (M2)

```jsonc
{ "input": { "type": "object", "required": ["objective","workspace_path","trust_profile"], "properties": {
    "objective":   { "type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 32768 },      // maxLength: proposed target
    "workspace_path": { "type": "string" },                  // canonicalized, under allowed_roots (FR-POL-10)
    "constraints": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" }, "maxItems": 64 },
    "trust_profile": { "$ref": "#/$defs/trust_profile" },    // above client max -> PERMISSION_BLOCKED (FR-MCP-06)
    "verification_commands": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" }, "minItems": 1 } }, // argv arrays, no shell (M3)
    "routing": { "$ref": "#/$defs/routing" },
    "priority": { "enum": ["interactive","normal","batch"], "default": "normal" },  // Proposed
    "wait_seconds": { "type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 60, "default": 0 }, // max 60: proposed target
    "idempotency_key": { "type": "string", "minLength": 8, "maxLength": 128 },
    "privileged_scope": { "$ref": "#/$defs/privileged_scope" } } },  // M3; required iff trust_profile is privileged (§3.1)
  "output": "envelope (status is current state; terminal only if the run finished within wait_seconds)" }
```

Behaviour: validation, path and profile checks, then in one transaction insert `meta_session` (new), `run` (`QUEUED`, or
`AWAITING_APPROVAL` for `privileged`, with its `PENDING` approval row, §3.1) and the first `run_event`; only then return
or wait (§10). A repeated `(client, idempotency_key)` with identical `request_hash` returns the original run; the same
key with different parameters fails `INVALID_REQUEST` (FR-MCP-08). `wait_seconds` is a bounded wait on run completion;
it never holds a DB transaction and returns the current non-terminal envelope on expiry.

**`continue_task`** (M2 same provider, M5 cross-provider)

```jsonc
{ "input": { "required": ["meta_session_id","instruction"], "properties": {
    "meta_session_id": {"type":"string"}, "instruction": {"type":"string","maxLength":32768},
    "routing": {"$ref":"#/$defs/routing"},      // different provider => handoff bundle (FR-SES-04)
    "trust_profile": {"$ref":"#/$defs/trust_profile"},   // default: previous run's; may not exceed client max;
                                                         // privileged only with the meta-session's approval (§3.1)
    "wait_seconds": {"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":60} } },
  "output": "envelope" }
```

*Reconciled in M1, applied in T2.4:* an earlier draft gave `continue_task` an `idempotency_key`, which conflicts with
[FRD §7.2](../requirements/FRD.md#72-continue_task) C6 and FR-MCP-08 (keys for `delegate_task` only). The FRD wins: the
published schema (`src/mcp/schemas.ts`) has no key, so a key is rejected as an unknown property (`INVALID_REQUEST`).
Adding it back needs an SRS change first.

Fails `INVALID_REQUEST` if the session has a non-terminal run holding the lease.

*M5 (Proposed, Delegated, [ADR-0038](../adr/0038-handoff-bundle-and-continuity.md)):* the input schema above does not
change (no decisions or open-work input). BR-18 chooses resume or handoff, a cross-provider `privileged` continuation is
refused with `INVALID_REQUEST` while ROADMAP Q-61 is open, and a bundle that cannot be built is `TOOL_FAILURE` with
`details.reason = "HANDOFF_BUNDLE_UNAVAILABLE"`; steps in [§15.7](#157-continue_task-with-handoff).

**`get_run`** `{ run_id, include_events?: { after_seq, limit<=200 } }` returns the envelope plus optional compact events
(type and bounded payload; no raw output).
**`cancel_run`** `{ run_id, reason? }` returns the envelope; cancellation is asynchronous (WF-4) and the status may
still be non-terminal. Cancelling a terminal run is a no-op returning its envelope.
**`get_artifacts`** `{ run_id, kinds?: ["diff"|"test_report"|"changed_files"|"log_summary"|"handoff_view"|"pr_draft"|"handoff_condense"],
max_inline_bytes?: int }` returns `[{artifact_id, kind, sha256, size_bytes, inline_text?}]`; inline only when under
`max_inline_bytes` (default 65536, proposed target). `pr_draft` exists only when the PR-draft task produced an accepted
draft (M11, [§13.6](#136-task-contracts)); Switchboard never opens a pull request with it (FR-INN-11). `handoff_condense`
exists only when the handoff-condensation task stored one beside the raw `handoff_view` (M11, T11.10, [§13.6](#136-task-contracts));
it does not replace the view.
**`list_capabilities`** `{}` returns `{ schema_version, providers: [{provider, adapter_status, binary_version,
validated_range, auth_state, supported_profiles[], transports[], models[], sandbox_features[], git_write_control?}] }`;
reads cached preflight (NFR-PERF-02). `git_write_control` is `{ kind: "provider-deny-rules" | "none-documented", detail,
remaining_controls[] }`, the adapter's FR-GIT-01 declaration (Proposed, added in T2.2).
`supported_profiles` (T3.7, Proposed) is the provider's compile matrix on this host: a profile is listed only if the
host platform is verified for it (Linux only until M4; every profile counts as sandbox-dependent, NFR-PORT-02) and the
adapter's own `compile` of the resolved profile, with configured overrides, returns no `Ineligible` (the same check
the eligibility filter makes). `privileged` is listed only if the adapter compiles the narrowest scope (read-only
files, no shell, network off, repository configuration disabled, no bypass); each request's scope is still compiled
at admission and dispatch. An off-host profile is excluded from routing with the machine-readable reason
`PROFILE_PLATFORM_UNVERIFIED`, which maps to `ELIGIBILITY_NONE` (FRD BR-3).
**`get_capacity`** (M6) `{ provider? }` returns the FR-CAP-02 fields per provider with `source`; `explicit_remaining` is
null unless provider-reported. Detailed in [§16.3](#163-get_capacity-and-switchboard-capacity) (T6.1).
**`explain_route`** (M7) `{ run_id }` returns candidates, eligibility reasons, features, weights, scores and the
`affinity` object ([§3.2](#32-provider-and-model-affinity-m7)) from `route_decision`, no model reasoning. Detailed in
[§17.5](#175-explain_route) (T7.0).

**Project additions (M9, Proposed).** `delegate_task` gains an optional `project` (string, a configured project ID) and
`workspace_path` becomes optional; exactly one of the two must be present (FRD V12, FR-PRJ-03). `continue_task` takes no
project (it is fixed by the meta-session). The envelope gains `project_id` and, for runs that produced commits,
`commits: [{ "sha": string, "pushed": boolean }]`; it never contains messages or provenance detail (FR-MCP-04). New
read-only tool:

**`list_projects`** `{}` returns `{ projects: [{ id, name, environment_kind, contexts: [{ id, kind, push_policy }],
max_trust_profile?, allowed_providers? }] }` for the projects the calling client may use. It never returns paths outside
the allowed roots, credentials, remote URLs or image references beyond the digest (Proposed). For a client with
`manage_projects` (FR-PRJ-10, BR-32) three more tools exist; none is offered otherwise:

**`create_project`** `{ project: <§11.1 project object without paths outside allowed roots> }` returns
`{ id, revision, status: "ACTIVE" | "PENDING_APPROVAL", approval_needed?: string[] }`.
**`update_project`** `{ id, project }` writes a new revision under the same rules; a revision that widens the ceiling,
providers or push policy, or switches to `container`, is `PENDING_APPROVAL` and the previous approved revision stays in
force until the operator approves. **`delete_project`** `{ id }` marks the project `DELETED` unless a non-terminal run
uses it (`INVALID_REQUEST`). All three act only on projects created by the calling client; a configured project, or another
client's, returns `PERMISSION_BLOCKED`. Names and shapes are Proposed.

*As built (2026-10-13, Delegated, T9.2):* `delegate_task` publishes `workspace_path` and `project` as a root `oneOf`
(exactly one, else `INVALID_REQUEST` "exactly one of workspace_path and project is required"); `workspace_path` is no
longer in `required`. `continue_task` has no `project` input (a supplied one fails the closed schema,
`INVALID_REQUEST`). The envelope gains `project_id` (absent for a path run); `commits` waits for T9.3. `list_projects`
returns, per project the client lists in `mcp_clients[].projects`: `id`, `name`, `environment_kind`, `contexts[]`
(`id`, `kind`, `primary`, `push_policy`), `max_trust_profile?`, `effective_max_profile` (the lower of the client's and the
project's, **Proposed** addition) and `allowed_providers?` (config ids). It returns no path, remote, identity or image
reference. Errors: unknown project `INVALID_REQUEST`; project not in the client's list (or the client has no list)
`PERMISSION_BLOCKED`; profile above the effective ceiling `PERMISSION_BLOCKED` with `details.requested`,
`client_max_profile`, `project` and `effective_max_profile`; a `container` project `TOOL_FAILURE` with
`details.reason = "ENVIRONMENT_NOT_IMPLEMENTED"` before anything is written (M10; LLD §3 names no other code, so no new
code is added); base ref that does not resolve `INVALID_REQUEST`. Implementation: `src/mcp/server.ts`,
`src/mcp/schemas.ts`.

*As built (2026-10-13, Delegated, T9.9, [ADR-0030](../adr/0030-dynamic-project-revisions-and-approval.md)):* the three
tools are in `tools/list` only for a client with `manage_projects`; for any other client a call is answered exactly as
an unknown tool (`INVALID_REQUEST`) and recorded as a refused `project_event` (`TOOL_NOT_OFFERED`). Inputs:
`create_project { project }`, `update_project { id, project }` (`project.id` must equal `id`), `delete_project { id }`;
`project` is the configuration's `projectSchema` (§11.1) with Delegated bounds (strings without a pattern at most 4096
characters, arrays at most 32 items), closed, so a client-limit key such as `max_push_policy` is an unknown key. Result
(create, update, delete): `{ id, revision, status: "ACTIVE" | "PENDING_APPROVAL" | "DELETED", approval_needed?,
in_force_revision?, message? }` (`in_force_revision` and `message` Proposed). `approval_needed` values:
`container_environment`, `push_policy`, `commit_identity`, `commit_signing`, `widened_trust_ceiling`,
`widened_providers` (widening is measured against the revision in force). Errors, in check order: schema
`INVALID_REQUEST`; a control, separator or bidirectional character in any string `INVALID_REQUEST`
(`CONTROL_CHARACTER`); a configured id or another client's project `PERMISSION_BLOCKED` with one message for both
(`NOT_MANAGEABLE`); unknown id for update/delete, deleted id, or an existing id for create `INVALID_REQUEST` (ids are
never reused); the loader's structural rules `INVALID_REQUEST`; ceiling, provider or push above the client's
`PERMISSION_BLOCKED` (`CEILING_ABOVE_CLIENT`, `PROVIDER_OUTSIDE_CLIENT`, `PUSH_ABOVE_CLIENT`); quotas (32 live projects
per client, 100 revisions per project, Assumptions) `PERMISSION_BLOCKED`; filesystem: outside every allowed root (also
through `..` or a symlink) or inside the data directory `PERMISSION_BLOCKED`, not absolute, missing, not Git or
unresolvable `base_ref` `INVALID_REQUEST`, always with the field and a fixed text, never a resolved path. Deleting with
a non-terminal run, or while a `delegate_task`/`continue_task` for the project is being admitted, is `INVALID_REQUEST`
(`ACTIVE_RUN`; SRS FR-PRJ-10 and this section, read as winning over FRD BR-32's general "refusals are
`PERMISSION_BLOCKED`"). `list_projects` appends the client's own dynamic projects (not deleted, not shadowed by a
configured id) with `source: "dynamic"`, `revision` (the one in force, else the latest), `status`
(`ACTIVE | PENDING_APPROVAL | REJECTED`) and `pending_revision?`; configured entries are unchanged. Implementation:
`src/mcp/project-tools.ts`, `src/projects/`.

**Error envelope** (FR-MCP-07). Delivered as an MCP tool result with `isError: true`:

```jsonc
{ "error": { "code": "<taxonomy code>", "message": "human readable, redacted", "retryable": false,
             "run_id": "run_...", "details": { /* e.g. login_command, eligibility reasons, field path */ } } }
```

Mapping: schema/path failures `INVALID_REQUEST`; profile above client maximum `PERMISSION_BLOCKED`; a
`privileged_scope` with a non-`privileged` profile, `privileged` without one, or a `privileged` continuation without an
`APPROVED` approval for its meta-session and client, `INVALID_REQUEST` (§3.1); no eligible
provider `ELIGIBILITY_NONE` with per-provider reasons; an ineligible `require_provider` target returns the code mapped
from its eligibility reason per [FRD BR-3](../requirements/FRD.md#3-business-rules) (FR-MCP-05); unknown `run_id`
`INVALID_REQUEST`.

**Version gate and "no manifest" (M2 implementation note).** The version gate (`evaluateVersionGate`,
`src/adapters/manifest/gate.ts`) returns `ADAPTER_VERSION_UNVERIFIED` for an out-of-range or unparseable version and for
a missing manifest, and tells them apart only by `evidence.validatedRange` being `null` when there is no manifest. The
routing filter (`src/routing/eligibility.ts`) reads that field and reports `NO_MANIFEST` (failure code
`ELIGIBILITY_NONE`) when it is `null` and `ADAPTER_VERSION_UNVERIFIED` otherwise, as BR-3 requires. A shipped manifest
with an empty range (`<0.0.0`; Codex until 2026-10-13, see ROADMAP Q-12) is therefore `ADAPTER_VERSION_UNVERIFIED`, not
`NO_MANIFEST`; so is a version outside a non-empty range.

### 3.1 Privileged scope (M3)

Owner decisions 13 and 17 and [ADR-0017](../adr/0017-approved-privileged-scope-bypass.md). Shape, hash, lookup and
validation are Delegated (Proposed), not owner-approved. No code may emit a bypass flag until this contract and the
argv audit change (T3.17) are both in place. *Amended 2026-10-13, before merge:* the scope became machine-readable
(Delegated, ROADMAP Q-28 answered, ROADMAP §4.4) and the approval binding moved from one run to the meta-session (owner
decision 17, ROADMAP Q-29 answered). *Amended 2026-10-13* by owner decisions 19 and 22 (mechanism Delegated): a
client configured `privileged_approval: "not_required"` is admitted without the operator's `approve` (**Client policy**
below, [ADR-0019](../adr/0019-per-client-privileged-approval.md)), and the lifecycle no longer refuses every
